<script type="text/javascript">
	function trackAction(category, action, label, nonInteraction) {
		if(category.length > 0 && action.length > 0) {
			if(label == null || label === false) {
				label = '';
			}
			dataLayer.push({
				'event': 'trackedEvent',
				'eventCategory': category,
				'eventAction': action,
				'eventLabel': label,
				'eventNonInteraction': nonInteraction
			});
		}
	}

	$(document).ready(function() {
		$('.js-track').each(function() {
			$(this).click(function() {
				var category = $(this).attr('data-track-category');
				var action = $(this).attr('data-track-action');
				var label = $(this).attr('data-track-label');
				trackAction(category, action, label, 0);
			});
		});

		$('.js-track-submit').each(function() {
			$(this).submit(function() {
				var category = $(this).attr('data-track-category');
				var action = $(this).attr('data-track-action');
				var label = $(this).attr('data-track-label');
				trackAction(category, action, label, 0);
			});
		});

		$(document).on('submit', '.hubspot-track-submit form', function (e) {
			$tracker = $(this).closest('.hubspot-track-submit');
			var category = $tracker.attr('data-track-category');
			var action = $tracker.attr('data-track-action');
			var label = $tracker.attr('data-track-label');
			trackAction(category, action, label, 0);
		});
	});
</script>
<script type="text/javascript">
	function formsToDataLayer($form, label) {
		var formHash = {};
		var formArray = $form.serializeArray();
		var formEmail = '';

		for(i=0; i<formArray.length; i++){
			var a = formArray[i];
			var name = a.name;
			var value = a.value;
			if(name != 'hs_context') {
				formHash[name] = value;
			}
			if(name == 'email') {
				formEmail = value;
			}
		}

		dataLayer.push({
			event: 'formSubmissionParsing',
			formLabel: label,
			formEmail: formEmail,
			formValues: formHash
		});
	}
</script>

<div class="o-grid__span-12 o-grid__span-5@lg o-grid__offset-1@lg u-align-center aos-init" data-aos="fade" data-aos-duration="2000">
<h3 class="o-title--simplified o-title--lg u-margin-bottom-2 u-margin-right-3"><span class="o-heading--highlight">All-in-One</span> SaaS Commerce Platform</h3>
<p class="o-body u-margin-bottom-1 u-margin-right-4 u-margin-right-0@sm">Focus on your business, not your billing and back-office. Paddle unifies payments, subscription and billing management, manual invoicing, sales tax and financial compliance, fraud, and more with an All-in-One SaaS Commerce platform that grows with you.</p>

isAutoplaySupported(supported => {
    if (supported) {
        $('video').on('inview', function(event, isInView) {
            var video = $(this);
            
            
            var isPlaying = video.currentTime > 0 && !video.paused && !video.ended && video.readyState > 2;

            if (isInView && !isPlaying) {
                video.trigger('play');
            } else {
                video.trigger('pause');
            }
        });

    } else {
        $('video').each(function() {
            var fallbackImageSrc = $(this).data('fallback');
            var fallbackClass = $(this).data('fallback-class');

            if (fallbackImageSrc) {
                var fallbackImage = $("<img>", {src: fallbackImageSrc});
                if (fallbackClass) {
                    fallbackImage.addClass(fallbackClass);
                }
                $(this).replaceWith(fallbackImage);
            }
        });
    }
});

<script type="text/javascript">
    // Youtube video + analytics
var tag = document.createElement('script');

tag.src = "https://www.youtube.com/iframe_api";
var firstScriptTag = document.getElementsByTagName('script')[0];
firstScriptTag.parentNode.insertBefore(tag, firstScriptTag);

var player;
var videoId = $('.yt-player').attr('data-videoId');
var videoTitle = $('.yt-player').attr('data-title') + " (" + videoId + ")";

function onYouTubeIframeAPIReady() {
	window.videoPlayed = false;
	window.videoMilestoneHit = false;

	player = new YT.Player('yt-player', {
		height: '450',
		width: '700',
		videoId: videoId,
		playerVars: {
			autoplay: 0,
			controls: 1,
			loop: 0,
			modestbranding: 0,
			rel: 0,
			showinfo: 0
		},
		events: {
			'onReady': function(e) {},
			'onStateChange': function(e) {
				if(e.data == 1) {
					if(!window.videoPlayed) {
						window.videoPlayed = true;
						
							trackAction('Video', 'Viewed', videoTitle, 0);
						
					}
					
					if(!window.videoMilestoneHit) {
						window.videoPlaying = setInterval(function() {
							if(player.getPlayerState() == 1) {
								if(player.getCurrentTime() >= 30) {
									window.videoMilestoneHit = true;
									clearInterval(window.videoPlaying);
									
										trackAction('Video', 'Viewed 30 Seconds', videoTitle, 0);
									
								}
							} else {
								clearInterval(window.videoPlaying);
							}
						}, 1000);
					}
				} else {
					clearInterval(window.videoPlaying);
				}
			}
		}
	});

}
  </script>
